# Skills · S · part 47

859 entries, steward to story-reference. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **steward**: The long-running owner of ONE initiative or project. Use when asked to run, drive, coordinate or own a project, when a human comments inside a scope you hold, or when resuming from a handoff. Dispatches by launching `/relay-ticket &lt;TICKET&gt;` sessions, reading their reports, answering in threads, keeping status current. ([coalesce-labs/catalyst](https://github.com/coalesce-labs/catalyst/tree/HEAD/plugins/dev/skills/steward) · ★20)
- **steward**: Guia específica do GarraRUST para dirigir um PR até o verde — quais falhas de CI são ambientais, quais são suas, e o que validar antes de empurrar. Lida ao reagir a eventos de CI ou de review num PR. ([michelbr84/GarraRUST](https://github.com/michelbr84/GarraRUST/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/steward) · ★17)
- **steward**: Keep the vault true. Two jobs in one skill: capture (fold anything from the inbox or a finished run into the right wiki pages) and health-check (hunt contradictions, stale claims, orphan pages, and propose pruning). Run when the user says "file this", "add this to the vault", "process the inbox", "clean up the vault", "health-check my notes", or when bank, research, or recall hand something over for filing. A vault nobody maintains becomes a graveyard nobody trusts. ([machina-exm/co-founder](https://github.com/machina-exm/co-founder/tree/HEAD/skills/steward) · ★13 · 4 copies)

Use when founders are overthinking strategy at the expense of execution, when organisations are drowning in process without progress, when the gap between stated philosophy and daily behaviour needs closing, or when a team needs to cut through complexity and focus on what actually works. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/stoic-pragmatism) · ★52)
- **stoic-problem-solving**: Teaches Marcus Aurelius's central method for converting obstacles into advantages, covering the reverse clause, creative reframing, persistence under adversity, and the discipline of focused action. Use when a startup faces setbacks that feel terminal, when market conditions shift against you, when key plans fail and alternatives seem absent, or when the team needs a framework for resilience and resourcefulness under pressure. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/stoic-problem-solving) · ★52)
- **stoic-right-action**: Applies Stoic ethics of right action to entrepreneurial decision-making, covering the four cardinal virtues (courage, justice, temperance, wisdom), ethical leadership under pressure, and the discipline of choosing the harder right over the easier wrong. Use when facing ethical dilemmas in business, building a values-driven culture, making decisions with imperfect information, navigating conflicts between profit and principle, or establishing personal and organizational codes of conduct. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/stoic-right-action) · ★52)
- **stoic-self-awareness**: Applies Stoic practices of self-examination to entrepreneurial leadership, drawing on Seneca's evening review, Epictetus's teaching on honest self-assessment, and Marcus Aurelius's private journaling discipline. Covers blind spots, the gap between self-image and reality, integration of character, and continuous philosophical self-scrutiny. Use when suspecting blind spots in leadership, receiving feedback that contradicts self-image, seeking a structured introspection practice, struggling with integrity under pressure, or building habits of ongoing self-examination. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/stoic-self-awareness) · ★52)
- **stoic-setback-debrief**: Recover from a professional setback — a failed launch, brutal feedback, a public mistake, a lost deal, a layoff — using the actual exercises from Marcus Aurelius' Meditations: the control sort, the evening review, and turning the obstacle into the task. Use when someone says 'today went badly', 'I blew it', 'the launch failed', 'I got torn apart in that meeting', or before replying to something that stung. Produces a structured debrief that separates what happened from the story, and ends in one next action. ([mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills](https://github.com/mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/exports/openclaw/stoic-setback-debrief) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **stoic-setback-debrief**: Summarise what Stoic Setback Debrief does in one line. Use when asked to [trigger phrases the user would say]. Produces [the concrete artifact]. - **stoic-unbiased-thought**: Applies the Stoic discipline of objective perception to entrepreneurial thinking, drawing on Marcus Aurelius's practice of describing things in their simplest terms, the 'view from above' technique, and the Stoic method of stripping value judgments from events. Covers cognitive distortions through a Stoic lens, the difference between observation and interpretation, and the practice of seeing things as they are. Use when distorted thinking is driving poor decisions, when hype or panic is clouding judgment, when strong opinions need stress-testing, or when seeking objectivity amid emotionally c… ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/stoic-unbiased-thought) · ★52)
- **stoic-virtue-and-kindness**: Presents the Stoic argument that character is the ultimate competitive advantage and that virtue -- encompassing honesty, kindness, justice, and moral courage -- is not a means to success but constitutes success itself. Use when an entrepreneur faces ethical dilemmas, when competitive pressure tempts shortcuts, when building company culture around values rather than metrics, when kindness is dismissed as weakness in a high-stakes environment, or when seeking philosophical grounding for why doing right and doing well are the same endeavour. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/stoic-virtue-and-kindness) · ★52)

Covers a frame-delta timer that a long hold cannot bank progress against, why an empty onLongPress callback is what makes hold-then-release resume instead of navigate, reading the pager's target page rather than its current one inside a tap handler… ([maxrave-dev/kotlin-footguns](https://github.com/maxrave-dev/kotlin-footguns/tree/HEAD/skills/story-reel-auto-pager) · ★563)
- **story-reels**: Monta peças verticais 9:16 — story, reels, shorts, TikTok — respeitando as áreas seguras da interface. Use quando o usuário disser "story", "reels", "shorts", "vertical", "9:16", "TikTok", "capa de reels", "template de story". A restrição dominante aqui é a UI do app, que cobre topo, base e lateral. ([pwdev-solucoes/pwdev-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/pwdev-solucoes/pwdev-claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/pwdev-social-media/skills/story-reels) · ★2)
- **story-reference**: Use when correcting a storytelling or comedy misconception, looking up a runtime, act-length, page-count or industry figure, finding the books, or needing a quick-reference picker — plus the current state of the production contraction and AI in film and television. Companion to the other storytelling skills.
